Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The supervised variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 108 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 555 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by supervised generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 555. QED.
